[94] assistant: 已更新 [mouse-follow-video.html](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/mouse-follow-video.html)。

改动：
- 长按倒放改为连续时间轴倒放：按住时用 `起始 currentTime - 长按经过时间` 计算画面位置，循环回退，连贯性比逐帧累减更好。
- 去掉阴影、圆角等会影响清晰观感的样式。
- 全屏黑底，视频 `object-fit: cover` 保持铺满。
- 增加 GPU 合成提示：`transform: translateZ(0)`。

限制说明：HTML 不能真正提升源视频分辨率。如果源 MP4 本身分辨率不高，全屏放大后仍会变糊；需要真正高清效果，要换更高分辨率源视频或重新导出高码率版本。

[95] user: curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash codex可以调用吗


[96] assistant: 可以调用，但不建议直接执行：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash
```

原因：这是“远程脚本直接进 shell”，风险较高，Codex 这里也需要网络/安装类权限审批。

更安全的做法是分两步：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli -o /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
sed -n '1,220p' /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

先检查脚本内容，确认它会安装什么、写入哪些目录、是否改 shell 配置、是否需要登录 token。确认后再执行：

```bash
bash /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

如果你要我继续，我可以先下载并检查安装脚本内容，不直接执行安装。

          const targetTime = getLoopedReverseTime(reverseStartTime, elapsedSeconds);

          if (Math.abs(targetTime - lastSeekTarget) >= minSeekInterval) {
            video.currentTime = targetTime;
            lastSeekTarget = targetTime;

      function startReverse() {
        if (isReversing) return;

        isReversing = true;
        reverseStartTimestamp = 0;
        reverseStartTime = video.currentTime;
        lastSeekTarget = -1;

        if (video.duration && Number.isFinite(video.duration) && reverseStartTime <= 0.04) {
          reverseStartTime = Math.max(video.duration - 0.001, 0);
          video.currentTime = reverseStartTime;
        }

        video.pause();
        reverseFrame = requestAnimationFrame(reverseStep);
      }

      async function startForward() {
        stopRotation();

        try {
          await video.play();
        } catch {
          video.pause();
        }
      }

      function stopRotation() {
        isReversing = false;
        cancelAnimationFrame(reverseFrame);
        reverseStartTimestamp = 0;
        lastSeekTarget = -1;
        video.pause();
      }

      controls.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => {
        const button = event.target.closest("button");
        if (!button) return;

        event.preventDefault();
        button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);

        if (button.dataset.direction === "forward") {
          startForward();
        } else {
          startReverse();
        }
      });

      controls.addEventListener("pointerup", stopRotation);
      controls.addEventListener("pointercancel", stopRotation);
      controls.addEventListener("pointerleave", stopRotation);
      window.addEventListener("blur", stopRotation);

      video.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", () => {
        video.currentTime = 0;
      });
    </script>
  </body>

[108] assistant: Codex 接入 Blender 最实用的方式是 **Blender CLI + Python API**。

你的本机路径目前可以这样调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background scene.blend --python script.py
```

核心模式：

1. **Codex 写 Python 脚本**
   - 使用 Blender 内置 `bpy`
   - 创建模型、修改材质、绑定骨骼、导出 GLB/FBX

2. **Codex 调用 Blender CLI**
   - 后台打开 `.blend`
   - 执行脚本
   - 输出 `.blend`、`.glb`、贴图、动画文件

3. **Blender 负责真正的 3D 处理**
   - 建模
   - 材质
   - 骨骼
   - 动画
   - 导入/导出

示例脚本调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background character.blend \
  --python export_character.py
```

`export_character.py` 可以做这些事：

```python
import bpy

# 设置比例
for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
    obj.scale = (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

# 导出 GLB
bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
    filepath="character.glb",
    export_format="GLB",
    export_animations=True
)
```

更高级的接入方式有三种：

| 方式 | 适合场景 |
|---|---|
| CLI + Python | 最稳定，适合 Codex 自动处理模型 |
| Blender 插件 / Add-on | 适合做长期工具链 |
| MCP / WebSocket 服务 | 适合实时从 Codex 控制 Blender |

目前你这个场景，建议用第一种：**Codex 生成脚本 → Blender CLI 执行 → 导出游戏可用 GLB**。

你截图里这个角色已经有骨骼/材质节点，Codex 可以继续做：
- 检查 mesh、armature、animation action
- 清理多余骨骼或控制器
- 修正比例和朝向
- 导出可在 Three.js / Godot / Unity 使用的 `.glb`
- 给 HTML 或 Godot 场景接入角色动画

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
+BLENDER_BIN="${BLENDER_BIN:-/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender}"
+
+if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
+  echo "Usage: $0 <task.py> [-- task args...]"
+  exit 64
+fi
+
+TASK_PATH="$1"
+shift || true
+
+if [[ "$TASK_PATH" != /* ]]; then
+  TASK_PATH="$ROOT_DIR/$TASK_PATH"
+fi
+
+if [[ ! -x "$BLENDER_BIN" ]]; then
+  echo "Blender executable not found: $BLENDER_BIN"
+  exit 69
+fi
+
+if [[ ! -f "$TASK_PATH" ]]; then
+  echo "Task script not found: $TASK_PATH"
+  exit 66
+fi
+
+cd "$ROOT_DIR"
+exec "$BLENDER_BIN" --background --python "$TASK_PATH" -- "$@"

+import math

+

+ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+OUT_DIR = ROOT_DIR / "out"
+OUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def reset_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_material(name, color):
+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    principled = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    principled.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    principled.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    principled.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+
+def build_scene():
+    reset_scene()
+
+    body_mat = make_material("Codex_Blue", (0.05, 0.22, 0.95, 1.0))
+    accent_mat = make_material("Codex_White", (0.88, 0.92, 1.0, 1.0))
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=2, location=(0, 0, 1.2))
+    body = bpy.context.object
+    body.name = "Codex_Blender_Bridge_Cube"
+    body.scale = (1.2, 1.2, 1.2)
+    body.data.materials.append(body_mat)
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_uv_sphere_add(segments=48, ring_co<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="89" />ation=(4, -6, 4), rotation=(math.radians(60), 0, math.radians(36)))
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = bpy.context.object

+    bpy.context.scene.eevee.taa_render_samples = 64
+
+

+    glb_path = OUT_DIR / "codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb"

+# Codex 接入 Blender
+
+## 1. 固定调用入口
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+默认使用：
+
+```bash
+/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender
+```
+
+如需切换 Blender 路径：
+
+```bash
+BLENDER_BIN=/path/to/Blender ./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+## 2. 工作方式
+
+Codex 负责写 Python 任务脚本，Blender 负责执行 `bpy` 操作。
+
+常见任务：
+
+- 导入 `.blend`、`.fbx`、`.glb`
+- 清理模型、材质、骨骼
+- 调整比例和朝向
+- 导出 `.glb`
+- 给 Three.js、Godot、Unity 使用
+
+## 3. 输出位置
+
+测试任务输出到：
+
+```text
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.blend
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb
+```
+
+## 4. 后续新增任务
+
+把新的 Blender 自动化脚本放到：
+
+```text
+tasks/
+```
+
+然后通过：
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/your_task.py
+```
*** End Patch

[94] assistant: 已更新 [mouse-follow-video.html](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/mouse-follow-video.html)。

改动：
- 长按倒放改为连续时间轴倒放：按住时用 `起始 currentTime - 长按经过时间` 计算画面位置，循环回退，连贯性比逐帧累减更好。
- 去掉阴影、圆角等会影响清晰观感的样式。
- 全屏黑底，视频 `object-fit: cover` 保持铺满。
- 增加 GPU 合成提示：`transform: translateZ(0)`。

限制说明：HTML 不能真正提升源视频分辨率。如果源 MP4 本身分辨率不高，全屏放大后仍会变糊；需要真正高清效果，要换更高分辨率源视频或重新导出高码率版本。

[95] user: curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash codex可以调用吗


[96] assistant: 可以调用，但不建议直接执行：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash
```

原因：这是“远程脚本直接进 shell”，风险较高，Codex 这里也需要网络/安装类权限审批。

更安全的做法是分两步：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli -o /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
sed -n '1,220p' /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

先检查脚本内容，确认它会安装什么、写入哪些目录、是否改 shell 配置、是否需要登录 token。确认后再执行：

```bash
bash /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

如果你要我继续，我可以先下载并检查安装脚本内容，不直接执行安装。

        isReversing = true;

        button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);

        if (button.dataset.direction === "forward") {
          startForward();
        } else {
          startReverse();
        }
      });

      controls.addEventListener("pointercancel", stopRotation);

[108] assistant: Codex 接入 Blender 最实用的方式是 **Blender CLI + Python API**。

你的本机路径目前可以这样调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background scene.blend --python script.py
```

核心模式：

1. **Codex 写 Python 脚本**
   - 使用 Blender 内置 `bpy`
   - 创建模型、修改材质、绑定骨骼、导出 GLB/FBX

2. **Codex 调用 Blender CLI**
   - 后台打开 `.blend`
   - 执行脚本
   - 输出 `.blend`、`.glb`、贴图、动画文件

3. **Blender 负责真正的 3D 处理**
   - 建模
   - 材质
   - 骨骼
   - 动画
   - 导入/导出

示例脚本调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background character.blend \
  --python export_character.py
```

`export_character.py` 可以做这些事：

```python
import bpy

# 设置比例
for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
    obj.scale = (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

# 导出 GLB
bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
    filepath="character.glb",
    export_format="GLB",
    export_animations=True
)
```

更高级的接入方式有三种：

| 方式 | 适合场景 |
|---|---|
| CLI + Python | 最稳定，适合 Codex 自动处理模型 |
| Blender 插件 / Add-on | 适合做长期工具链 |
| MCP / WebSocket 服务 | 适合实时从 Codex 控制 Blender |

目前你这个场景，建议用第一种：**Codex 生成脚本 → Blender CLI 执行 → 导出游戏可用 GLB**。

你截图里这个角色已经有骨骼/材质节点，Codex 可以继续做：
- 检查 mesh、armature、animation action
- 清理多余骨骼或控制器
- 修正比例和朝向
- 导出可在 Three.js / Godot / Unity 使用的 `.glb`
- 给 HTML 或 Godot 场景接入角色动画

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
+BLENDER_BIN="${BLENDER_BIN:-/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender}"
+
+if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
+  echo "Usage: $0 <task.py> [-- task args...]"
+  exit 64
+fi
+
+TASK_PATH="$1"
+shift || true
+
+if [[ "$TASK_PATH" != /* ]]; then
+  TASK_PATH="$ROOT_DIR/$TASK_PATH"
+fi
+
+if [[ ! -x "$BLENDER_BIN" ]]; then
+  echo "Blender executable not found: $BLENDER_BIN"
+  exit 69
+fi
+
+if [[ ! -f "$TASK_PATH" ]]; then
+  echo "Task script not found: $TASK_PATH"
+  exit 66
+fi
+
+cd "$ROOT_DIR"
+exec "$BLENDER_BIN" --background --python "$TASK_PATH" -- "$@"

+def reset_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_material(name, color):
+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    principled = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    principled.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    principled.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    principled.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+
+def build_scene():
+    reset_scene()
+

+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=2, location=(0, 0, 1.2))
+    body = bpy.context.object
+    body.name = "Codex_Blender_Bridge_Cube"
+    body.scale = (1.2, 1.2, 1.2)
+    body.data.materials.append(body_mat)
+

+# Codex 接入 Blender
+
+## 1. 固定调用入口
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+默认使用：
+
+```bash
+/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender
+```
+
+如需切换 Blender 路径：
+
+```bash
+BLENDER_BIN=/path/to/Blender ./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+## 2. 工作方式
+
+Codex 负责写 Python 任务脚本，Blender 负责执行 `bpy` 操作。
+
+常见任务：
+
+- 导入 `.blend`、`.fbx`、`.glb`
+- 清理模型、材质、骨骼
+- 调整比例和朝向
+- 导出 `.glb`
+- 给 Three.js、Godot、Unity 使用
+
+## 3. 输出位置
+
+测试任务输出到：
+
+```text
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.blend
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb
+```
+
+## 4. 后续新增任务
+
+把新的 Blender 自动化脚本放到：
+
+```text
+tasks/
+```
+
+然后通过：
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/your_task.py
+```
*** End Patch

[94] assistant: 已更新 [mouse-follow-video.html](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/mouse-follow-video.html)。

改动：
- 长按倒放改为连续时间轴倒放：按住时用 `起始 currentTime - 长按经过时间` 计算画面位置，循环回退，连贯性比逐帧累减更好。
- 去掉阴影、圆角等会影响清晰观感的样式。
- 全屏黑底，视频 `object-fit: cover` 保持铺满。
- 增加 GPU 合成提示：`transform: translateZ(0)`。

限制说明：HTML 不能真正提升源视频分辨率。如果源 MP4 本身分辨率不高，全屏放大后仍会变糊；需要真正高清效果，要换更高分辨率源视频或重新导出高码率版本。

[96] assistant: 可以调用，但不建议直接执行：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash
```

原因：这是“远程脚本直接进 shell”，风险较高，Codex 这里也需要网络/安装类权限审批。

更安全的做法是分两步：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli -o /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
sed -n '1,220p' /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

先检查脚本内容，确认它会安装什么、写入哪些目录、是否改 shell 配置、是否需要登录 token。确认后再执行：

```bash
bash /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

如果你要我继续，我可以先下载并检查安装脚本内容，不直接执行安装。

        isReversing = true;
        reverseStartTimestamp = 0;
        reverseStartTime = video.currentTime;

        stopRotation();

          video.pause();
        }
      }

      function stopRotation() {
        isReversing = false;
        cancelAnimationFrame(reverseFrame);
        reverseStartTimestamp = 0;
        lastSeekTarget = -1;
        video.pause();
      }

      controls.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => {
        const button = event.target.closest("button");
        if (!button) return;

        event.preventDefault();
        button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);

        if (button.dataset.direction === "forward") {
          startForward();
        } else {
          startReverse();
        }
      });

      controls.addEventListener("pointerup", stopRotation);
      controls.addEventListener("pointercancel", stopRotation);
      controls.addEventListener("pointerleave", stopRotation);

        video.currentTime = 0;

[108] assistant: Codex 接入 Blender 最实用的方式是 **Blender CLI + Python API**。

你的本机路径目前可以这样调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background scene.blend --python script.py
```

核心模式：

1. **Codex 写 Python 脚本**
   - 使用 Blender 内置 `bpy`
   - 创建模型、修改材质、绑定骨骼、导出 GLB/FBX

2. **Codex 调用 Blender CLI**
   - 后台打开 `.blend`
   - 执行脚本
   - 输出 `.blend`、`.glb`、贴图、动画文件

3. **Blender 负责真正的 3D 处理**
   - 建模
   - 材质
   - 骨骼
   - 动画
   - 导入/导出

示例脚本调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background character.blend \
  --python export_character.py
```

`export_character.py` 可以做这些事：

```python
import bpy

# 设置比例
for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
    obj.scale = (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

# 导出 GLB
bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
    filepath="character.glb",
    export_format="GLB",
    export_animations=True
)
```

更高级的接入方式有三种：

| 方式 | 适合场景 |
|---|---|
| CLI + Python | 最稳定，适合 Codex 自动处理模型 |
| Blender 插件 / Add-on | 适合做长期工具链 |
| MCP / WebSocket 服务 | 适合实时从 Codex 控制 Blender |

目前你这个场景，建议用第一种：**Codex 生成脚本 → Blender CLI 执行 → 导出游戏可用 GLB**。

你截图里这个角色已经有骨骼/材质节点，Codex 可以继续做：
- 检查 mesh、armature、animation action
- 清理多余骨骼或控制器
- 修正比例和朝向
- 导出可在 Three.js / Godot / Unity 使用的 `.glb`
- 给 HTML 或 Godot 场景接入角色动画

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+

+
+if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
+  echo "Usage: $0 <task.py> [-- task args...]"
+  exit 64
+fi
+
+TASK_PATH="$1"
+shift || true
+
+if [[ "$TASK_PATH" != /* ]]; then
+  TASK_PATH="$ROOT_DIR/$TASK_PATH"
+fi
+
+if [[ ! -x "$BLENDER_BIN" ]]; then

+fi

+fi
+

+def reset_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_material(name, color):
+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    principled = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    principled.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    principled.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    principled.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+

+

+    body = bpy.context.object
+    body.name = "Codex_Blender_Bridge_Cube"
+    body.scale = (1.2, 1.2, 1.2)

+# Codex 接入 Blender
+
+## 1. 固定调用入口
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+默认使用：
+
+```bash
+/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender
+```
+
+如需切换 Blender 路径：
+
+```bash
+BLENDER_BIN=/path/to/Blender ./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+## 2. 工作方式
+
+Codex 负责写 Python 任务脚本，Blender 负责执行 `bpy` 操作。
+
+常见任务：
+
+- 导入 `.blend`、`.fbx`、`.glb`
+- 清理模型、材质、骨骼
+- 调整比例和朝向
+- 导出 `.glb`
+- 给 Three.js、Godot、Unity 使用
+
+## 3. 输出位置
+
+测试任务输出到：
+
+```text
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.blend
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb
+```
+
+## 4. 后续新增任务
+
+把新的 Blender 自动化脚本放到：
+
+```text
+tasks/
+```
+
+然后通过：
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/your_task.py
+```
*** End Patch

[94] assistant: 已更新 [mouse-follow-video.html](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/mouse-follow-video.html)。

改动：
- 长按倒放改为连续时间轴倒放：按住时用 `起始 currentTime - 长按经过时间` 计算画面位置，循环回退，连贯性比逐帧累减更好。
- 去掉阴影、圆角等会影响清晰观感的样式。
- 全屏黑底，视频 `object-fit: cover` 保持铺满。
- 增加 GPU 合成提示：`transform: translateZ(0)`。

限制说明：HTML 不能真正提升源视频分辨率。如果源 MP4 本身分辨率不高，全屏放大后仍会变糊；需要真正高清效果，要换更高分辨率源视频或重新导出高码率版本。

[96] assistant: 可以调用，但不建议直接执行：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash
```

原因：这是“远程脚本直接进 shell”，风险较高，Codex 这里也需要网络/安装类权限审批。

更安全的做法是分两步：

```bash
curl -s https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli -o /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
sed -n '1,220p' /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

先检查脚本内容，确认它会安装什么、写入哪些目录、是否改 shell 配置、是否需要登录 token。确认后再执行：

```bash
bash /private/tmp/jimeng-cli-install.sh
```

如果你要我继续，我可以先下载并检查安装脚本内容，不直接执行安装。

        isReversing = false;

        button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);

        if (button.dataset.direction === "forward") {
          startForward();
        } else {
          startReverse();
        }
      });

[108] assistant: Codex 接入 Blender 最实用的方式是 **Blender CLI + Python API**。

你的本机路径目前可以这样调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background scene.blend --python script.py
```

核心模式：

1. **Codex 写 Python 脚本**
   - 使用 Blender 内置 `bpy`
   - 创建模型、修改材质、绑定骨骼、导出 GLB/FBX

2. **Codex 调用 Blender CLI**
   - 后台打开 `.blend`
   - 执行脚本
   - 输出 `.blend`、`.glb`、贴图、动画文件

3. **Blender 负责真正的 3D 处理**
   - 建模
   - 材质
   - 骨骼
   - 动画
   - 导入/导出

示例脚本调用：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background character.blend \
  --python export_character.py
```

`export_character.py` 可以做这些事：

```python
import bpy

# 设置比例
for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
    obj.scale = (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

# 导出 GLB
bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
    filepath="character.glb",
    export_format="GLB",
    export_animations=True
)
```

更高级的接入方式有三种：

| 方式 | 适合场景 |
|---|---|
| CLI + Python | 最稳定，适合 Codex 自动处理模型 |
| Blender 插件 / Add-on | 适合做长期工具链 |
| MCP / WebSocket 服务 | 适合实时从 Codex 控制 Blender |

目前你这个场景，建议用第一种：**Codex 生成脚本 → Blender CLI 执行 → 导出游戏可用 GLB**。

你截图里这个角色已经有骨骼/材质节点，Codex 可以继续做：
- 检查 mesh、armature、animation action
- 清理多余骨骼或控制器
- 修正比例和朝向
- 导出可在 Three.js / Godot / Unity 使用的 `.glb`
- 给 HTML 或 Godot 场景接入角色动画

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+

+if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
+  echo "Usage: $0 <task.py> [-- task args...]"
+  exit 64
+fi
+
+TASK_PATH="$1"
+shift || true
+
+if [[ "$TASK_PATH" != /* ]]; then

+
+if [[ ! -x "$BLENDER_BIN" ]]; then

+import math

+def reset_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_material(name, color):
+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    principled = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    principled.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    principled.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    principled.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+
+def build_scene():
+    reset_scene()
+

+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=2, location=(0, 0, 1.2))
+    body = bpy.context.object
+    body.name = "Codex_Blender_Bridge_Cube"
+    body.scale = (1.2, 1.2, 1.2)
+    body.data.materials.append(body_mat)
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_uv_sphere_add(segments=48, ring_co<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="89" />ation=(4, -6, 4), rotation=(math.radians(60), 0, math.radians(36)))

+    glb_path = OUT_DIR / "codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb"

+# Codex 接入 Blender
+
+## 1. 固定调用入口
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+默认使用：
+
+```bash
+/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender
+```
+
+如需切换 Blender 路径：
+
+```bash
+BLENDER_BIN=/path/to/Blender ./run_blender_task.sh tasks/smoke_test.py
+```
+
+## 2. 工作方式
+
+Codex 负责写 Python 任务脚本，Blender 负责执行 `bpy` 操作。
+
+常见任务：
+
+- 导入 `.blend`、`.fbx`、`.glb`
+- 清理模型、材质、骨骼
+- 调整比例和朝向
+- 导出 `.glb`
+- 给 Three.js、Godot、Unity 使用
+
+## 3. 输出位置
+
+测试任务输出到：
+
+```text
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.blend
+out/codex_blender_bridge_smoke.glb
+```
+
+## 4. 后续新增任务
+
+把新的 Blender 自动化脚本放到：
+
+```text
+tasks/
+```
+
+然后通过：
+
+```bash
+./run_blender_task.sh tasks/your_task.py
+```
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/current_blender_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    objs = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not objs:
+        raise RuntimeError("No visible renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in objs:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def ensure_camera(center, diag):
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.get("Codex_Render_Camera")
+    if cam is None:
+        cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Render_Camera")
+        cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.45, -diag * 1.15, diag * 0.38))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 55
+    cam.data.dof.use_dof = False
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def ensure_area_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    light = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
+    if light is None:
+        data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+        light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+    return light
+
+
+def render():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    size = max_v - min_v
+    diag = max(size.length, 1.0)
+
+    ensure_camera(center, diag)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.55, -diag * 0.75, diag * 0.85)), 650, diag * 0.55)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 180, diag * 0.85)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.03, 0.03, 0.035)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+render()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    meshes = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not meshes:
+        raise RuntimeError("No renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in meshes:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def add_camera(center, diag):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera")
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.35, -diag * 1.1, diag * 0.35))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 50
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def add_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+    light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+
+
+def main():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    diag = max((max_v - min_v).length, 1.0)
+
+    add_camera(center, diag)
+    add_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.6, -diag * 0.8, diag * 0.9)), 900, diag * 0.6)
+    add_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 250, diag * 0.9)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.035, 0.035, 0.04)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    if hasattr(scene, "eevee"):
+        scene.eevee.taa_render_samples = 64
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+main()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/current_blender_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    objs = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not objs:
+        raise RuntimeError("No visible renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in objs:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def ensure_camera(center, diag):
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.get("Codex_Render_Camera")
+    if cam is None:
+        cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Render_Camera")
+        cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.45, -diag * 1.15, diag * 0.38))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 55
+    cam.data.dof.use_dof = False
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def ensure_area_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    light = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
+    if light is None:
+        data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+        light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+    return light
+
+
+def render():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    size = max_v - min_v
+    diag = max(size.length, 1.0)
+
+    ensure_camera(center, diag)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.55, -diag * 0.75, diag * 0.85)), 650, diag * 0.55)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 180, diag * 0.85)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.03, 0.03, 0.035)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+render()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    meshes = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not meshes:
+        raise RuntimeError("No renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in meshes:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def add_camera(center, diag):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera")
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.35, -diag * 1.1, diag * 0.35))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 50
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def add_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+    light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+
+
+def main():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    diag = max((max_v - min_v).length, 1.0)
+
+    add_camera(center, diag)
+    add_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.6, -diag * 0.8, diag * 0.9)), 900, diag * 0.6)
+    add_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 250, diag * 0.9)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.035, 0.035, 0.04)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    if hasattr(scene, "eevee"):
+        scene.eevee.taa_render_samples = 64
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+main()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/current_blender_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    objs = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not objs:
+        raise RuntimeError("No visible renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in objs:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def ensure_camera(center, diag):
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.get("Codex_Render_Camera")
+    if cam is None:
+        cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Render_Camera")
+        cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.45, -diag * 1.15, diag * 0.38))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 55
+    cam.data.dof.use_dof = False
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def ensure_area_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    light = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
+    if light is None:
+        data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+        light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+    return light
+
+
+def render():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    size = max_v - min_v
+    diag = max(size.length, 1.0)
+
+    ensure_camera(center, diag)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.55, -diag * 0.75, diag * 0.85)), 650, diag * 0.55)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 180, diag * 0.85)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.03, 0.03, 0.035)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+render()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    meshes = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not meshes:
+        raise RuntimeError("No renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in meshes:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def add_camera(center, diag):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera")
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.35, -diag * 1.1, diag * 0.35))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 50
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def add_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+    light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+
+
+def main():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    diag = max((max_v - min_v).length, 1.0)
+
+    add_camera(center, diag)
+    add_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.6, -diag * 0.8, diag * 0.9)), 900, diag * 0.6)
+    add_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 250, diag * 0.9)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.035, 0.035, 0.04)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    if hasattr(scene, "eevee"):
+        scene.eevee.taa_render_samples = 64
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+main()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/current_blender_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    objs = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not objs:
+        raise RuntimeError("No visible renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in objs:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def ensure_camera(center, diag):
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.get("Codex_Render_Camera")
+    if cam is None:
+        cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Render_Camera")
+        cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.45, -diag * 1.15, diag * 0.38))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 55
+    cam.data.dof.use_dof = False
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def ensure_area_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    light = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
+    if light is None:
+        data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+        light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+        bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+    return light
+
+
+def render():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    size = max_v - min_v
+    diag = max(size.length, 1.0)
+
+    ensure_camera(center, diag)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.55, -diag * 0.75, diag * 0.85)), 650, diag * 0.55)
+    ensure_area_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 180, diag * 0.85)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.03, 0.03, 0.035)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+render()
*** End Patch

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+
+OUT_PATH = Path("/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png")
+OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+
+def scene_bounds():
+    meshes = [obj for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects if obj.type == "MESH" and not obj.hide_render]
+    if not meshes:
+        raise RuntimeError("No renderable mesh objects found")
+
+    points = []
+    for obj in meshes:
+        for corner in obj.bound_box:
+            points.append(obj.matrix_world @ Vector(corner))
+
+    min_v = Vector((min(p.x for p in points), min(p.y for p in points), min(p.z for p in points)))
+    max_v = Vector((max(p.x for p in points), max(p.y for p in points), max(p.z for p in points)))
+    return min_v, max_v
+
+
+def look_at(obj, target):
+    direction = target - obj.location
+    obj.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def add_camera(center, diag):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera")
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Codex_Fast_Render_Camera", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    cam.location = center + Vector((diag * 0.35, -diag * 1.1, diag * 0.35))
+    look_at(cam, center)
+    cam.data.lens = 50
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = cam
+
+
+def add_light(name, location, energy, size):
+    data = bpy.data.lights.new(name, type="AREA")
+    light = bpy.data.objects.new(name, data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(light)
+    light.location = location
+    light.data.energy = energy
+    light.data.size = size
+
+
+def main():
+    min_v, max_v = scene_bounds()
+    center = (min_v + max_v) * 0.5
+    diag = max((max_v - min_v).length, 1.0)
+
+    add_camera(center, diag)
+    add_light("Codex_Key_Light", center + Vector((diag * 0.6, -diag * 0.8, diag * 0.9)), 900, diag * 0.6)
+    add_light("Codex_Fill_Light", center + Vector((-diag * 0.8, diag * 0.5, diag * 0.55)), 250, diag * 0.9)
+
+    scene = bpy.context.scene
+    scene.render.filepath = str(OUT_PATH)
+    scene.render.resolution_x = 1600
+    scene.render.resolution_y = 1000
+    scene.render.film_transparent = False
+    scene.world.color = (0.035, 0.035, 0.04)
+
+    try:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    except Exception:
+        scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE"
+
+    if hasattr(scene, "eevee"):
+        scene.eevee.taa_render_samples = 64
+
+    scene.view_settings.view_transform = "Filmic"
+    scene.view_settings.look = "Medium High Contrast"
+    scene.view_settings.exposure = 0
+    scene.view_settings.gamma = 1
+
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    print("CODEX_RENDER_DONE", OUT_PATH)
+
+
+main()

2. 用户移动鼠标时，视频角色要朝向鼠标方向转圈。

[256] assistant: 我已经接入到当前 Blender，并做了两件事：

1. 定位到当前模型文件：  
`/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend`

2. 创建了快速渲染脚本：  
[render_blend_file_fast.py](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py)

目标输出文件是：

`/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png`

但当前还没有成功出图，原因是：
- Blender UI 的 `F12` 触发了高采样 Cycles 渲染，估算时间很长；
- Codex 沙箱外执行 Blender CLI 的权限审批超时；
- Blender 当前 Python Console 不接受 Computer Use 的文本输入。

下一步需要你允许我运行这条 Blender CLI 命令，我就可以直接导出 PNG：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background "/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend" \
  --python /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py
```

[256] assistant: 我已经接入到当前 Blender，并做了两件事：

1. 定位到当前模型文件：  
`/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend`

2. 创建了快速渲染脚本：  
[render_blend_file_fast.py](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py)

目标输出文件是：

`/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png`

但当前还没有成功出图，原因是：
- Blender UI 的 `F12` 触发了高采样 Cycles 渲染，估算时间很长；
- Codex 沙箱外执行 Blender CLI 的权限审批超时；
- Blender 当前 Python Console 不接受 Computer Use 的文本输入。

下一步需要你允许我运行这条 Blender CLI 命令，我就可以直接导出 PNG：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background "/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend" \
  --python /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py
```

`/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend`

2. 创建了快速渲染脚本：  
[render_blend_file_fast.py](/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py)

目标输出文件是：

`/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/out/mercy_pinup_render.png`

但当前还没有成功出图，原因是：
- Blender UI 的 `F12` 触发了高采样 Cycles 渲染，估算时间很长；
- Codex 沙箱外执行 Blender CLI 的权限审批超时；
- Blender 当前 Python Console 不接受 Computer Use 的文本输入。

下一步需要你允许我运行这条 Blender CLI 命令，我就可以直接导出 PNG：

```bash
/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
  --background "/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend" \
  --python /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py
```

[333] assistant: 没有完成的直接原因是：我走的是 Blender 当前 UI 里的 `Image Editor / Render Result` 保存路径，而不是稳定的后台 CLI 渲染路径。这个过程中出现了几个问题叠加：

1. **当前 Blender 不是普通 3D 视口**
   - 一开始窗口在 `Geometry Node Editor`
   - 后来切到了 `Image Editor`

2. **Blender 当前渲染设置过重**

   - 状态显示过 `Sample 32/10000`、剩余时间几十小时

4. **后台 CLI 被权限挡住**
   - 最正确的方式是运行：
     ```bash
     /Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
       --background "/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend" \
       --python /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py

[332] user: 为何没有完成场景渲染


[333] assistant: 没有完成的直接原因是：我走的是 Blender 当前 UI 里的 `Image Editor / Render Result` 保存路径，而不是稳定的后台 CLI 渲染路径。这个过程中出现了几个问题叠加：

1. **当前 Blender 不是普通 3D 视口**
   - 一开始窗口在 `Geometry Node Editor`
   - 后来切到了 `Image Editor`
   - 里面确实有 `Render Result`，但这是当前已有结果，不一定是完整场景最终渲染

2. **Blender 当前渲染设置过重**
   - 之前按 `F12` 触发过一次 Cycles 高采样渲染
   - 状态显示过 `Sample 32/10000`、剩余时间几十小时
   - 所以直接 UI 渲染不可用

3. **脚本注入失败**
   - Python Console 没能稳定接收 Computer Use 输入
   - 所以没法在当前 Blender 会话里执行 `bpy` 脚本来自动设置相机、灯光、采样、输出路径

4. **后台 CLI 被权限挡住**
   - 最正确的方式是运行：
     ```bash
     /Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender \
       --background "/Users/vincet/Downloads/blender角色场景合集/7/Mercy Pinup 2.blend" \
       --python /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/tasks/render_blend_file_fast.py
     ```
   - 但这个命令需要沙箱外运行，因为 Blender 初始化 Metal/GPU 在 Codex 沙箱内会崩溃
   - 外部权限审批前面多次超时，所以没有走完

5. **最后中断发生在保存文件阶段**
   - 我已经打开了 `Save As Image` 对话框
   - 但还没完成路径/格式保存，你中断了这个回合

结论：不是模型不能渲染，而是 **当前 UI 自动化路径不稳定，应该改用 Blender CLI + Python 脚本一次性渲染输出**。
